## Runner feelings (persona cautious = careful planner, iPad 1180x820; harness c826eca4→8dc395ba (5 same-life resumes at buổi 308/424/813/1424/2149); Build changed twice mid-life: old LIVE → 4763d410aa0d → 13f84c70f2a3)
- y3 (rank 2, Quý nhân) · shots m-new-309-*: DELIGHTED: this is the first life in my set that climbs (rank 0 → 2 → later 3); Chuẩn bị thị tẩm cards appear and the favour loop finally gives me something to plan.
- y3–y14 · «Sang ngày mới» tapped 3× no turn (×56) and «Phụ tuyến · Tân quân lập uy · Chương 2 · Hồi 1/1» no reachable button (stuck ×15 min ×4) · FRUSTRATED: the same two traps as in every other life; each cost the life 15+ minutes and the player a reload.
- y4–y14 · «Tâm trạng · <person>» dialog queue (2 copies, later Q 7) and «Sổ nguyệt sự» with no close/next at the end (×7 loops) · FRUSTRATED/CONFUSED: I cannot close the monthly ledger; no button after scrolling to the end.
- y6–y12 · rank 3 stretch · DELIGHTED/BORED mix: favour events and «Việc nhờ» give variety, then the loop repeats; 14 years in total feels long for one life.
- y14 age 31 · ending: «Bà đỡ quỳ ngoài điện: ngươi khó sinh, chỉ giữ được một. Hoàng thượng lặng đi hồi lâu rồi phán: “Giữ con.” Ngươi gắng sinh hạ hài nhi rồi nhắm mắt.» · DELIGHTED: the best ending line of the set — emotional, with the Emperor's one-word choice; it follows from my road (pregnancy). I felt it.
